# DFCI script and data catalogue

Generated by `tools/squirrel/catalog.py dfci <data dir>`; do not edit by hand. JSON: `dfci_files.json`, `dfci_names.json`. Translations are machine (`"tr":"machine"`) until reviewed; JP is kept beside them.
